												local function pool()
													local ok6, result6 = pcall(EggState.ReadFieldEggs)
													if not ok6 or type(result6) ~= "table" or type(result6.Records) ~= "table" then
														return {}
													end
													local v158, position3 = fn137()
													position3 = position3 and position3.Position or v150
													local minvalue = recoveredLocals24.ui.minvalue and recoveredLocals24.ui.minvalue.get() or recoveredLocals24.v70[118]
													local v159 = clock()
													local tbl63 = {}
													local tbl64 = {}

													for _, record in pairs(result6.Records) do
														if record.Uid then
															tbl64[record.Uid] = true
														end

														local bottomCFrame = record.State == "Slot" and record.Uid and record.BottomCFrame

														if bottomCFrame then
															bottomCFrame = (tbl52[record.Uid] or 0) < v159
														end

														if bottomCFrame and record.Uid:sub(1, 13) ~= "FirstAreaEgg_" and not fn172(record) and fn166(record.AreaId) then
															local v160 = fn160(record)

															if minvalue <= v160 then
																tbl63[#tbl63 + 1] = {
																	uid = record.Uid,
																	area = record.AreaId,
																	nest = record.NestId,
																	cat = record.AssetCategory,
																	pos = record.BottomCFrame.Position,
																	score = v160,
																	dist = (record.BottomCFrame.Position - position3).Magnitude,
																}
															end
														end
													end

													for k in pairs(tbl54) do
														if not tbl64[k] then
															tbl54[k] = nil
														end
													end

													local mode = recoveredLocals24.ui.mode and recoveredLocals24.ui.mode.get() or "Best Value"

													if mode == "Nearest" then
														table.sort(tbl63, function(arg, arg2)
															return arg.dist < arg2.dist
														end)
													elseif mode == "Value Per Trip" then
														table.sort(tbl63, function(arg, arg2)
															return arg.score / (recoveredLocals24.v70[58] + arg.dist / 1200) > arg2.score / (recoveredLocals24.v70[58] + arg2.dist / 1200)
														end)
													else
														table.sort(tbl63, function(arg, arg2)
															if arg.score == arg2.score then
																return arg.dist < arg2.dist
															end
															return arg.score > arg2.score
														end)
													end

													return tbl63
												end
